## Review

**8 out of 10, Strong.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a strong, consistent identity (dark green and cream, neon accent, bold caps titles, rounded cards, section tabs and a clear agenda on slide 2), and the route from advantage to method to fund strategy to a short close is easy to follow. The detail slides are disciplined: slide 6 gives each fund a uniform card with a clear metric stack, slide 7 pairs a clean bar chart with two aligned tables, and slide 13 uses consistent card treatments with logos as proof. Weaknesses are consequential, though: several pages carry very small body text, long paragraphs and logo walls (slides 8, 20, 21, 31), and several pages show black redaction boxes or empty black video panels (slides 17, 20, 21, 22, 25, 29), which look unfinished. Slide 6 mixes TVPI and DPI by fund, and the footers are tiny, so some of the data is hard to compare.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the visual system, the section tabs and agenda on slide 2, and the card-based tables of slides 6, 7 and 13.

**Most useful repair:** Remove or replace the black redaction boxes and empty video panels on slides 17, 20, 21, 22, 25 and 29, and enlarge the body text on the dense slides.

**What would change the score:** Cut the paragraph copy and logo walls on slides 8, 12, 20, 21 and 31 down to one claim each, and make the metric sets on slide 6 consistent across funds.

_Confidence medium. 33 of 33 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck is a fundraising presentation from the venture firm Seedcamp, seeking investment for its Seedcamp VII (Core) and Seedcamp Nation II (Select) funds. It opens with a title slide and a headline on backing European tech founders, then lists the two fund offerings with their target sizes. The main sections follow in order: The Seedcamp Advantage (history, performance and team), The Seedcamp Way (sourcing, selecting and winning, supporting, and driving returns), and the fund strategies with their thesis, ticket sizes, ownership targets and follow-on reserves for each fund. The deck closes with a short call to join the Seedcamp Nation. Slides worth studying include the performance table comparing fund vintages, the power-law returns breakdown by outcome bucket, the sourcing channel slide showing how deals arrive, and the side-by-side fund strategy comparison. Several slides use compact tables and short labelled callouts, which make dense fund data easy to scan.

## Questions about this deck

**What fund sizes is Seedcamp seeking for Seedcamp VII (Core) and Seedcamp Nation II (Select) in its deck?**

Slide 2 lists Seedcamp VII (Core) at £165m/$220m and Seedcamp Nation II (Select) at £70m/$95m. These are the two fund offerings the deck presents for investment.

**What does the Seedcamp deck say about its power law of returns?**

Slide 7 says Seedcamp's Core strategy has 54% of returns coming from the >50x outcome bucket, from 12% of invested cost, and its Select strategy has 68% of returns from the 10x+ bucket. The slide notes these returns are illustrative, based on weighted Seedcamp returns 2008-2025.

**What startup conversion rates does Seedcamp report in its deck?**

Slide 8 shows a 79% pre-seed to seed conversion rate and a 39% seed to Series A conversion rate. It compares the seed to Series A figure with Europe's average of 19%.

**How is the Seedcamp fundraising deck structured?**

Slide 2 divides the deck into three sections: The Seedcamp Advantage (slides 3-10), The Seedcamp Way (slides 11-22), and The Opportunity to Invest (slides 23-32). The deck begins with a title slide and closes with a call to join the Seedcamp Nation.

Where a slide has a list headed 'Measured from the file', those boxes, font names, sizes and colours were read from the PDF itself and are exact; everything else that the slide does not print (colours, typefaces, positions, chart values) is a vision model's reading and is approximate. ### Slide 4

**Text**

HISTORY
PERFORMANCE
TEAM
WE’VE BEEN EUROPE’S / FIRST BELIEVERS / FOR NEARLY 20 YEARS
Since 2007, we’ve been backing Europe’s best talent from / Day One. With the expertise and network built from 560+ / companies and 1,000+ founders, we support entrepreneurs / through the highs and lows of company building.
Our distinctive team culture and long-term approach / make us founders’ strongest ally, unafraid to challenge / them in private when it matters most. 18 years in, we / continue to back winners and drive global success stories.
“Seedcamp took a chance on us back in 2015 / when we were a team of just ten people in / Romania. I trust the team implicitly when it / comes to uncovering and investing in new / categories and am delighted to support the / next generation of entrepreneurial talent to / come out of Seedcamp.”
Daniel Dines
Co-founder and CEO
UiPath
“Seedcamp was an instrumental player in / helping Revolut grow from a small-time / startup into a global banking alternative. / Hungry founders with ambitious plans / should begin their journey at Seedcamp.”
Nik Storonsky

Revolut

4

**Layout**

Two-column slide. Left column holds the top navigation, a three-line uppercase title, two paragraphs and two side-by-side dark quote cards at the bottom. Right column holds a large dark rounded panel with a 4x5 grid of logo tiles. A thin vertical black line with a leaf icon runs near the right edge, with a rotated footer label and the page number below. A footnote sits under the grid, right-aligned.

**Visuals**

Right panel: 4 columns by 5 rows of logo tiles. Row 1 has the '$1bn+' label in the first cell, then cream tiles for UiPath (orange and white logo), Wise (dark green logo) and Revolut (black wordmark). Rows 2 and 3 are cream tiles with logos: 9fin (blue), Pleo (black), synthesia (dark blue/black with a blue icon), FluidStack (black), iz.ai (blue), sorare (black), Function (faded orange), wefox (purple). Row 4 has the '$100M+' label in the first cell, then muted green-grey tiles: Metaview, Orbital (orange icon), Light. Row 5: a multicolour icon tile, Lindus Health (orange), buynomics (magenta and blue). Row 6: yonder, Primer (coral icon), ROSSUM (teal O), Ramp. Row 7: maze, juro (blue), resistant.ai, Flow. Two quote cards in dark green with rounded corners, each with a portrait photo at the bottom right: Daniel Dines (a bearded man with dark hair in a dark navy jacket) and Nik Storonsky (a blond man in a white shirt). A bright mint green band crosses the lower part of each card, with a cream rounded label holding the UiPath and Revolut logos. A small dark green circle with a mint leaf icon sits at the top right on the vertical line.

**Design**

Background: Cream #F7EEE5. Warm cream background #F7EEE5 with dark forest green #1E352D for titles and body text (body at 80% opacity). Title is a bold geometric sans-serif in uppercase, tightly spaced, about 6% of slide height. Body text is a rounded geometric sans (Gilroy-like) at about 2.5% of slide height. Accent mint green #1EF0A0 for the '$1bn+' and '$100M+' labels and the speaker names. The outer panel has rounded corners, with muted green-grey #507267 tiles in the lower area. The slide has rounded corners.

**Layout**

Top-left navigation tabs, with the SEEDCAMP wordmark beneath at left and a round green logo icon at the top right. Two horizontal rows of cream polaroid-style cards run off the left and right edges, each row joined by green connector bars, with the upper row at y 17-51% and the lower row at y 55-89%. Cards vary in width, with a year centred under each photo.

**Visuals**

Timeline of 15 cream cards (11 distinct photos, some cropped at the edges), each with a Seedcamp logo (serif wordmark with a green leaf) at the top, a photo, and a year beneath. Top row, left to right: 2007 card, a crowd of people standing on a street outside a yellow brick building with hanging flower baskets (cropped at the left edge); 2009 card, a young man in a white t-shirt and a woman at a table with papers next to a white Seedcamp banner, red wall with a Microsoft sign; 2011 card, a man in a dark jacket, a smiling woman in a red scarf and a man in a navy blazer, all with name badges; 2011 card, a woman presenting in front of a projector screen reading Seedhack to a seated audience in a red room; 2011 card, two men standing, one in a pink shirt holding papers, the other in a beige shirt; a final partly visible card at the right edge shows a man in a grey beanie and dark coat pointing (cropped). Bottom row, left to right: a partly visible card at the left edge showing a close-up of a man with dark hair and a grey t-shirt, in front of a building (cropped); 2018 card, a group of about 12 people standing in front of gold foil SEEDCAMP balloon letters; 2017 card, four people on a rooftop with a city skyline behind them, wearing blue shirts; 2017 card, a group of about 11 people sitting and standing on pale wooden hexagonal steps; 2016 card, a bald man with glasses and dark sweater with arms crossed, next to a smiling woman with long dark hair (cropped at the right edge). Bright green horizontal connector bars (about #00F5A0) join the cards at the mid-height of the photos. Top right: a green circle with a dark leaf icon.

**Design**

Background: Dark green #1E352D with rounded corners (black showing at the corners). Dark forest green background with rounded slide corners. Cards are cream #F7EEE5 with rounded corners of about 1% of the slide width. Navigation text is white, small sans-serif, with thin vertical divider ticks between items; HISTORY is bolder than the others. The wordmark is bright mint green #00F5A0 in a condensed, high-contrast serif display face. Years are dark green #1E352D in a geometric sans-serif with medium weight, about 3.5% of the slide height. Photos have slightly rounded corners.

### Slide 6

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Places each fund's vintage, size, investment count, MOIC, TVPI, DPI and IRR in a consistent card layout, so the fund records can be compared at a glance.
- Review: The detail slides are disciplined: slide 6 gives each fund a uniform card with a clear metric stack, slide 7 pairs a clean bar chart with two aligned tables, and slide 13 uses consistent card treatments with logos as proof.
- Review: Slide 6 mixes TVPI and DPI by fund, and the footers are tiny, so some of the data is hard to compare.
- Worth keeping: Keep the visual system, the section tabs and agenda on slide 2, and the card-based tables of slides 6, 7 and 13.
- What would change the score: Cut the paragraph copy and logo walls on slides 8, 12, 20, 21 and 31 down to one claim each, and make the metric sets on slide 6 consistent across funds.

**Layout**

Top navigation tabs at upper left, large all-caps title beneath at left margin. Below it a 4x2 grid of rounded cards: top row four dark green fund cards (Fund I-IV), bottom row two dark green cards (Fund V, VI) and two cream outlined cards (Select 2015-2022, Select SC Nation I). Each card has the title and stats list at left and a column of three logo tiles at right. Footnote definitions in a row below the grid. A thin vertical dark green line near the right edge with a leaf icon circle at top, rotated 'FUNDRAISING DECK 2025' text and page number 6 at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Eight rounded-rectangle cards. Six are dark green (#1E352D) filled; two are cream with thin dark green outlines. Each card contains three logo tiles (cream on dark cards, white on outlined cards) holding company logos: MyBuilder (white on dark purple badge with green icon), Codility (black wordmark), Stupeflix (red/pink icon and text), WISE (dark green with bright green flash), ELLIPTIC (black bold wordmark), GRABCAD (dark wordmark with red CAD), UiPath (orange with bracket logo), Revolut (black wordmark), Pleo (black stacked letters), synthesia (dark text with blue icon), FluidStack (black with layered icon), sorare (black with circle star icon), Lindus Health (orange/yellow circle with orange text), yonder (dark Y icon with text), Light (black triangle icon with text), umony (blue swoosh with dots), MURPHY (purple italic), SUNRISE (white text on red bar), Function (orange sphere with faded text), 9fin (blue bold). Thin horizontal divider lines separate stat rows. A dark green circle with a bright green leaf icon sits at the top right on the vertical line. Slide has rounded corners with black behind.

**Design**

Background: Solid cream #F7EEE5 with rounded slide corners over black. Warm cream background #F7EEE5. Title in dark green #1E352D, bold wide extended sans-serif, all caps. Fund names in bright mint green #1FE08F (extended sans). Stats in light cream sans-serif with labels in medium weight and values in regular. Outlined cards use dark green text. Cards have rounded corners (about 1.5% of slide), thin divider lines in lighter cream tone, no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 7

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Pairs two power-law bar charts with tiered tables of company counts, invested cost and returns share, showing the return distribution in one view.
- Review: The detail slides are disciplined: slide 6 gives each fund a uniform card with a clear metric stack, slide 7 pairs a clean bar chart with two aligned tables, and slide 13 uses consistent card treatments with logos as proof.
- Worth keeping: Keep the visual system, the section tabs and agenda on slide 2, and the card-based tables of slides 6, 7 and 13.

**Layout**

Top-left breadcrumb nav, then a large two-line left-aligned uppercase title. Below, a large dark green rounded panel holds two cream rounded cards side by side: left a bar chart, right two tables. A thin vertical dark green line runs down the right margin with a leaf icon at the top, rotated footer text and page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Left card: clustered bar chart, y-axis 'Return multiple on investments (x)' 0 to 12 in steps of 2 with the top label 12 beside the axis title, x-axis 'Year(s) since investment' with categories 1 to 9+ labelled with years 2024, 2023, 2022, 2021, 2020, 2019, 2018, 2017, 2007-2016. Legend: bright mint green square 'Core fund data', dark teal-grey square 'Select fund data'. Each category has a mint Core bar and a grey-green Select bar. Approximate values (Core/Select): yr1 1.2/1.0, yr2 1.3/1.1, yr3 1.6/1.8, yr4 1.8/1.1, yr5 2.9/2.7, yr6 4.4/3.7, yr7 4.4/5.6, yr8 6.6/5.1, yr9+ 12 (clipped, labelled 26x with black up arrow)/11. Right card: two tables. Core strategy table has grey-green header row (columns blank, <1x, 1–3x, 3–10x, 10x-50x, >50x), rows No of Co’s (110 target): 53, 29, 12, 12, 5; % of invested cost (pale mint highlighted row): 36%, 21%, 13%, 18%, 12%; % of returns: 1%, 5%, 8%, 32%, 54%. Select strategy table has the same style, header columns 0-1x, 1–3x, 3–10x, 10x+, rows No of Co’s (25 target): 5, 7, 8, 6; % of invested cost (mint highlighted): 12%, 22%, 35%, 31%; % of returns: 1%, 6%, 25%, 68%. Dark green circle with a mint leaf icon at top right on the vertical line.

**Design**

Background: Solid cream #F7EEE5. Cream background #F7EEE5 with rounded slide corners, a dark forest green #1E352D rounded panel, cream cards. Title in dark green #1E352D, bold wide geometric sans-serif capitals (similar to Syne/Gilroy extended), about 5.5% slide height per line. Card headings in black rounded geometric sans (medium weight, ~3.5% height). Body and table text in black Inter-like sans-serif ~1.9% height. Table headers grey-green #507267 with off-white #FBFAF5 text, highlight rows pale mint #CCFFEE, chart mint #00FFAA and grey-green #507267. Footnote tiny grey #A6A6A6. No shadows or gradients.

### Slide 8

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential, though: several pages carry very small body text, long paragraphs and logo walls (slides 8, 20, 21, 31), and several pages show black redaction boxes or empty black video panels (slides 17, 20, 21, 22, 25, 29), which look unfinished.
- What would change the score: Cut the paragraph copy and logo walls on slides 8, 12, 20, 21 and 31 down to one claim each, and make the metric sets on slide 6 consistent across funds.

**Layout**

Left two-thirds hold a 6-column table with 16 rows under a two-line uppercase title; the top-left carries a small tab navigation. Right column stacks two dark green rounded cards (bar chart above, conversion rates below). A thin vertical dark green line runs down the right edge with a round green leaf icon on it, a rotated deck label and page number at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Table of 16 funding rounds with company logos in column 1, lead investor logos in column 4, and country flag icons in column 6 (UK, Denmark, France, Sweden, Germany, Switzerland, Czech Republic). Horizontal thin dark rules separate rows, with a thicker rule under the header. Fluidstack's Amount and Lead investor cells are covered by black redaction rectangles. Bar chart 'Raised across Seedcamp co's $m' with mint green bars for 2016-2025, y-axis 0-900 in steps of 100. Approximate values: 2016 about 130, 2017 about 215, 2018 about 460, 2019 about 660, 2020 capped at 900 with an up arrow and label $2.1bn, 2021 capped at 900 with arrow and $6bn, 2022 capped at 900 with arrow and $1.6bn, 2023 about 445, 2024 about 575, 2025 about 715. Faint horizontal gridlines. Conversion card has three donut gauges in mint green on dark: 79% (almost a full ring), 39% (semicircle gauge), 19% (semicircle gauge), small calendar icons beside the Avg. months lines, and a dealroom.co logo at bottom right. Circular green leaf icon at top right on the vertical line.

**Design**

Background: Solid cream #F7EEE5 with rounded slide corners over black. Warm cream background #F7EEE5. Title in heavy dark green #1E352D, all caps geometric sans, about 5.5% of slide height per line. Table text dark green #1E352D in a rounded sans (Figtree-like), 1.7% for round/amount and 1.5% for one-liners. Dark green cards #1E352D with rounded corners of about 1% of height, no shadow. Accent mint green #02FDA8 for bars, chart title and card headings, donuts. Light cream text on cards. Slide corners are rounded with a black backdrop visible at corners.

**Layout**

Top breadcrumb nav at the left margin, large two-line left-aligned title below it. A 2x2 grid of dark green cards sits on the left (about 4.4% to 49.6% across) and a large team photo occupies the right (50.4% to 91.1%), with equal gutters. A rounded-outline strip of 18 logo tiles runs full width beneath. A thin vertical dark green line with a leaf icon sits at the right edge, with rotated footer text and page number below it.

**Visuals**

Group photograph of about 16 people, standing at the back and seated or perched in front on a blue sofa, in a bright studio-like room with a white wall, a paned window at top right, a plant at left and a dark wood floor. Smiling, most wearing dark navy, black and white smart casual clothes. Soft natural light. A horizontal neon mint green band crosses the photo behind the standing people at about 40.7-42.7% from the top. The photo has slightly rounded corners and is boxed. Four small mint line icons at the top right of each card: handshake with heart (Stability), head-profile or ear silhouettes (High-EQ), robot/gift-like icon (Grow), network of connected nodes (Lean). A dark green circle at top right (x about 94.5-96.5%, y about 8-12%) holds a mint leaf icon. A thin vertical dark green line runs down the right side at about 95.6%. The logo strip has 18 white rounded tiles, each with a logo: Penn shield, INSEAD, Doughty Hanson (green), Forbes 30 Under 30 (black), EY (yellow and black), Forbes Midas List (red), NYSE (blue), vodafone (red), a blue and red circle 3i, esade (black), Babson (dark), Wharton (blue), BCG (green), Fuse (orange), University of Cambridge (red shield), Meta (blue), Y Combinator (orange-red square with white Y), Adobe (red).

**Design**

Background: Solid cream #F7EEE5 with a thin dark green vertical rule at the right. Warm cream background #F7EEE5 with rounded slide corners. The title is in dark forest green #1E352D, an extended bold geometric sans-serif in uppercase, about 6% of the slide height. Card headings are mint #02FDA8 in a wide geometric sans (Syne/Unbounded-like) at medium weight, about 3%. Card body is cream #F7EEE5 in a light sans (Figtree/Gotham-like) at 1.74% of the height. Cards are dark green #1E352D with small rounded corners about 0.5% and no shadow. The logo strip has a thin dark green outline, rounded corners, a cream fill and white tiles.

Full-width slide with a breadcrumb at top left and a two-line left-aligned all-caps title below. The left half holds a two-column grid of four rows of Investment Team members, the right half a two-column grid of Platform + Ops members. Two rounded outlined boxes sit at bottom left, a colour legend runs along the bottom, an advisors block sits at bottom right, and a thin vertical line on the right edge carries a leaf icon, rotated footer text and the page number.

**Visuals**

Circular headshot photos beside each name: 8 investment team (bright mint-green rings for the first 7, Devin Hunt with a grey-green ring), 11 platform and ops (dark teal-blue rings). Small US flag icons next to Reshma Sohoni's Founded line, Hilary Howe's Joined line and the Associate - Select new hire. Small arrow icons (diagonal down-right) after the section headings. A green circular leaf icon at the top right on the vertical line. A black-and-white street map thumbnail in the New London Office box with a green dot marker and a red Underground roundel. Advisor logos: light green 'hfl' square, RSM with green/blue bar, purple Morgan Lewis wordmark. Legend dots: mint green, grey-green, dark teal. Thin vertical divider line between the two halves. Thick dark-green vertical line near the right edge.

**Design**

Background: Solid cream #F7EEE5. Warm cream background #F7EEE5. Title and headings in dark forest green #1E352D, a wide geometric sans-serif in bold caps for the title, regular for the section headings. Names in dark green, roles in medium green #3E7A63, join dates in dark green small regular sans. Accents mint green #1EF2A0 and teal #25607A. Boxes have thick dark green outline #1E352D with rounded corners and cream fill. The slide has rounded corners.

### Slide 11

**Text**

SEEDCAMP
Lindus Health
Sano
Synthesia
Sorare
The Seedcamp Way, powered by our Network

**Layout**

Dark full-bleed slide with the Seedcamp wordmark at top left and a round leaf icon at top right. A horizontal neon green band crosses the slide at about 40% height, running behind four cream polaroid-style photo cards of different sizes and heights. The Lindus Health card is cropped at the left edge and the Sorare card at the right edge. The title sits bottom left, left-aligned, spanning about 82% of the width.

**Visuals**

Four cream polaroid-style cards with rounded corners, each holding a team photo with a caption beneath. The horizontal neon green band (#02FDA8) passes behind or through the cards. In the Lindus and Sorare photos the band overlaps the people (cut-out effect), and in the Sano and Synthesia photos it shows through behind the people. 1) Lindus Health: three men standing on a rooftop terrace with a city skyline behind, in navy, dark green and puffer jackets; cropped by the left edge. 2) Sano: three people in a bright office, a man sitting on a rust-coloured sofa in black, a man standing in a dark shirt and a woman in a white blouse with jeans. 3) Synthesia: two men walking side by side down a corridor, one in a navy sweatshirt and dark trousers, the other in a pale green sweatshirt and light jeans, laughing. 4) Sorare: two men in dark navy jackets standing in front of a dark doorway with a green-painted lower wall; cropped by the right edge. A small round neon green icon with a dark leaf glyph sits top right.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark forest green #1E352D with rounded slide corners. Dark forest green background #1E352D. The Seedcamp wordmark and the highlighted part of the title are in neon mint green #02FDA8. The first part of the title and the card captions are cream/dark. The title is in a wide geometric sans-serif (Syne-like) in regular weight, about 7% of slide height. The wordmark is a condensed serif in caps. The cards are cream #F7EEE5 with rounded corners of about 1% of slide width and the photos inside have small rounded corners. Captions are about 3% of slide height in dark charcoal. No shadows or gradients.

**Text**

THE POWER OF THE SEEDCAMP NATION FLYWHEEL, / OUR UNFAIR ADVANTAGE
Ever growing founder network

12

**Layout**

Title top-left in two lines spanning most of the width. Left column holds four stacked outlined cards of equal size with an icon and heading, then body text. Right has a large dark green rounded panel containing a flywheel diagram. A thin vertical dark green line runs near the right edge with a round leaf icon at the top, rotated footer text and page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Four small dark green icons beside card headings: handshake/heart, target with arrow, buildings with bar chart, group of people. Right panel diagram: a horizontal bright mint green band runs left to right with three circled white arrows, linking cream boxes 'We back the best at Day One' (leaf icon), 'Grows ...' (mint box with dark green text), 'Category defining companies' and 'Top Exits' (exit door icon). 'Epic Talent' (people icon) sits top centre and 'Investment & commercial partnerships' (money icon) sits bottom centre. Thin white arrow lines form loops: from Epic Talent left and down to 'We back the best' and down to the 'Grows' box; from 'Grows' up to Epic Talent; 'Talent Migration' line from Top Exits up and left to Epic Talent with arrowhead; a line from Category defining companies down to Investment with arrowhead; a 'Repeat Founders' line from Top Exits along the bottom to the 'We back the best' box with arrowhead upward, and a branch into Investment. Small white logo tiles above Category defining companies: synthesia, PLEO, Revolut, FluidStack; below Top Exits: UiPath, wise; below Repeat Founders: a multicolour logo tile and ezra. Round dark green leaf icon at top right.

**Design**

Background: Solid cream #F7EEE5. Cream background #F7EEE5 with rounded slide corners. Title in dark green #1E352D, bold wide geometric sans-serif capitals, about 5.5% of slide height. Card headings in dark green medium geometric sans (Syne-like/Gilroy), about 2.2% height. Card body in near-black regular sans about 1.5% height. Cards cream with thin dark green rounded outline, radius about 1%. Diagram panel dark green #1E352D with rounded corners; boxes cream with small radius; accent mint #02FDA8; white 1px connector lines. No shadows or gradients.

### Slide 13

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Uses a grid of labelled sourcing channels, each with a bold heading and a short explanation, to make the deal flow mechanics easy to scan.
- Review: The detail slides are disciplined: slide 6 gives each fund a uniform card with a clear metric stack, slide 7 pairs a clean bar chart with two aligned tables, and slide 13 uses consistent card treatments with logos as proof.
- Worth keeping: Keep the visual system, the section tabs and agenda on slide 2, and the card-based tables of slides 6, 7 and 13.

**Text**

SOURCING | SELECTING & WINNING | SUPPORTING | DRIVING RETURNS
WE SEE EUROPE’S BEST COMPANIES FIRST – THROUGH OUR NETWORK, OUR SYSTEMS AND OUR BRAND
SC Founder Network & Partners
Our founders are plugged into local founder communities across the continent and refer new companies to us constantly. Our founders are our staunchest advocates - we have not lost a deal that has been referred to us by a Seedcamp founder in 5+ years.
Lindus Health
rerun.io
medusa
Lead Investor Referrals
Lead investors frequently invite us into rounds they are leading. They recognise the power of the Seedcamp brand and network for their companies. Our lower ownership strategy allows us to work hand-in-hand with other funds in the ecosystem.
buynomics
source.dev
Light
Outbound
We have built systems to identify top talent early via automating tracking of public signals. Given the reach of the Seedcamp Nation, we are often able to get direct and warm introductions to the best founders starting new companies before they fundraise.
hideout
Witty Machines
Pelikan
Angel & Emerging Manager Network
We have strong ties to the best angels and Emerging Managers in Europe. As a result of our brand and collaborative strategy, we are often brought deals to lead from angels and micro funds. We regularly share deal flow, host events and partner deeply with this community.
MURPHY
linkup
Prelude
Cold inbound
Founders can upload submissions directly to our website to receive funding. It is a smaller source of dealflow for us but we still continue to find amazing opportunities via this channel.
autone
liveflow
BioOrbit
Second-time Seedcamp founders
Founders we’ve previously backed, with whom we’ve built deep relationships over the years, come back to us to invest in their new companies. This is happening at an accelerating rate given the size of our portfolio.
spektr
Arcjet
SUNRISE
Talent from the Seedcamp portfolio
As our portfolio companies mature, some of the best entrepreneurial talent take their experiences with them to start companies of their own. The founders are often familiar with us, sometimes personally, or have warm intros from their founders.
TILT
ferry
Motley
FUNDRAISING DECK 2025
13

**Layout**

Cream slide with a 4x2 grid: the large uppercase headline occupies the top-left cell, and seven rounded cards fill the remaining cells (3 across the top right, 4 across the bottom). Each card has a heading, a paragraph, and a row of three logo tiles at the bottom. A top navigation bar sits at top-left. A thin vertical green line runs down the right side with a leaf icon at its top, a rotated footer text and the slide number at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Seven rounded-rectangle cards alternating dark green filled (SC Founder Network, Outbound, Angel & Emerging Manager Network, Second-time Seedcamp founders) and cream with a thin black outline (Lead Investor Referrals, Cold inbound, Talent from the Seedcamp portfolio). Each card contains three rounded logo tiles, cream on dark cards and white with thin dark outline on light cards. Logos: Lindus Health (orange and yellow circle icon), rerun.io, medusa, buynomics, source.dev, Light, hideout (purple/blue badge), Witty Machines, Pelikan, MURPHY (purple italic), linkup, Prelude, autone, liveflow (blue), BioOrbit, spektr, Arcjet, SUNRISE (red/orange badge), TILT, ferry, Motley. A thin dark green vertical line at the far right with a small dark green circle holding a green leaf icon at the top.

**Design**

Background: Solid cream #F7EEE5. Warm cream background #F7EEE5. Headline in dark green #1E352D, bold uppercase grotesque sans, about 4.5% of slide height. Dark cards #1E352D with bright mint-green headings #1FE08F and cream body text; light cards have black headings and black body text. Headings in a geometric sans (Space Grotesk-like), body in a light sans (Inter-like), about 1.9% of slide height. Card corner radius about 1.5% with thin black outlines on light cards; no shadows or gradients. Nav text is small grey sans with the active item SOURCING in dark.

**Text**

SOURCING | SELECTING & WINNING | SUPPORTING | DRIVING RETURNS
WE MOVE FAST, AND OBSESS OVER OPTIMISING / THE FOUNDER EXPERIENCE
Over the years, we’ve seen that / outlier founders share a few defining / traits, which we actively look for →
“Dissatisfaction with ‘good enough’, a relentless drive and sense of urgency, a / sharp insight into the problem they are tackling, a deep understanding of the / customer pain point, clarity of thought that inspires others to follow them, and / previous experience building something from 0-1, whether that’s a video game / at home, or an exited venture business.”
Sourcing / machine
Sourcing engine, / bringing in the most / ambitious founders / in Europe
Initial / Calls
Initial calls with one or / two investment team / members
Weekly / deal review
Deal review meetings / each week with full Inv. / team - ensuring / quickest possible turn- / around
Weekly / partner pitch
Batching IC meetings / together enables us to / benchmark companies / against each other, / identifying those with / the winning edge.
Investment
Investments into ±35 - / new companies each / year
Average time less than 2 weeks
FUNDRAISING DECK 2025
14

**Layout**

Top navigation row of four section labels, then a large two-line uppercase title at left. Below, a wide dark green banner holds a green statement at left and a cream quote box at right. Beneath it, a row of five equal cards joined by green circular arrows, with a bracket and caption underneath. A vertical line with a leaf badge runs down the right margin, with a rotated footer and page number at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Five process cards in a row: first and fifth are dark green filled with rounded corners, the middle three are cream with thin dark outlines and rounded corners. Four bright green circles with dark arrow icons sit on the junctions between cards. A dark bracket line below spans from the centre of card 2 to the centre of card 4. A dark green banner with rounded corners contains a cream rounded quote box. A small arrow follows the green statement. A vertical dark green line at the right edge carries a small dark green circle with a green leaf icon at the top. The slide has rounded corners at the top right.

**Design**

Background: Solid cream #F7EEE5. Cream background #F7EEE5. Title in dark green #1E352D, bold wide geometric sans-serif in capitals, about 6% of slide height. Banner statement and the titles of the dark cards in bright mint green #02FDA8, a wide geometric sans (Syne/Cabinet-like). Titles of the cream cards in black. Body text is light-weight sans-serif (Inter-like) at about 1.9% of slide height. Quote is italic light sans, dark green. Navigation labels are small grey-green with the active item (SELECTING & WINNING) darker. Cards have about 1% corner radius and thin 1px outlines; no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 15

**Text**

SOURCING
SELECTING & WINNING
SUPPORTING
DRIVING RETURNS
SEEING MORE SHARPENS OUR JUDGMENT, / AND ONCE COMMITTED WE GO ALL IN TO WIN
At the early stage we deal with a lot of unknowns, but by stacking new founders against the thousands we’ve seen before, we refine our judgment with every decision and consistently spot investment themes before the market.
We spot breakout themes early — often before the “hype”
Our proven wins in these sectors fuels even more top founders to seek our backing
FINTECH
2011- 2016
2020 -
HEALTH / BIO
2015 - 2018
2021 -
AI
2015 - 2020
2022 -
Why we win
Portfolio Founder love
We quickly build rapport with new founders, while our portfolio founders remain our strongest advocates and give powerful references.
Brand and Track record
We have a Tier 1 track record, with our brand tied to the earliest chapters of Europe’s top tech success stories. Trusted and well-liked across the ecosystem, founders want to team up with Seedcamp and be part of it.
Access to the Nation
From Day One, founders tap into a powerful peer network through WhatsApp groups and in-person events that fast-tracks learning and often converts into early customers. We provide access to experts and BD intros to drive early GTM in Europe/US, with hands-on support in fundraising, talent, and US expansion.
Investment Offering
By leading with no more than 10% equity, we offer founders a uniquely attractive deal. This leaves significant space to bring alongside us the best suited angels and co-investors.
Speed & Conviction
We move fast and with conviction, often deciding in under 2 weeks. We get on WhatsApp with founders early and work 24/7 to secure investment.
FUNDRAISING DECK 2025
15

**Layout**

Top navigation row of four section labels at left, large two-line uppercase title below spanning most of the width. Left column below holds an intro paragraph and a three-row logo grid (Fintech, Health/Bio, AI) with two time-period columns; right column holds a dark rounded panel titled 'Why we win' with five stacked cream cards. A thin vertical dark line runs near the right edge with a round leaf icon at top, rotated footer text and page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Grid of company logos separated by dark lines. Fintech 2011-2016: Wise, Pleo, Cuvva, 9fin, Revolut, wefox, Curve. Fintech 2020-: Primer, Light, spektr, yonder, mimo, Murphy. Health/Bio 2015-2018: a heart-shaped logo, thriva, Viz.ai, myrecovery, ezra, Sano. Health/Bio 2021-: Lindus Health, Sable Biotechnology, Inovia Bio, verisian, bookend.AI, Dalton. AI 2015-2020: Reinfer, synthesia, ROSSUM, aibuild, resistant.ai, FluidStack. AI 2022-: a multicolour block logo, linkup, rerun.io, BOTIKA, memories.ai, TXT AI Engineering. Dark green rounded panel with five cream rounded cards. Round dark green icon with a green leaf at top right. Thick vertical dark green line at right edge.

**Design**

Background: Cream #F7EEE5. Warm cream background #F7EEE5 with dark green #1E352D text and shapes. The title is a heavy wide geometric sans-serif in uppercase, in dark green, about 6% of slide height. Body text is a wide geometric sans (Syne-like or Cabinet Grotesk style) in regular weight. The panel heading 'Why we win' is bright green #12E88A. Card headings are medium-weight wide sans at about 3% height. Card body text is a small sans at about 1.5%. Panel corners are rounded about 2%. Lines are 2-3px dark green. The slide has rounded corners at top left and top right.

### Slide 17

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential, though: several pages carry very small body text, long paragraphs and logo walls (slides 8, 20, 21, 31), and several pages show black redaction boxes or empty black video panels (slides 17, 20, 21, 22, 25, 29), which look unfinished.
- Suggested repair: Remove or replace the black redaction boxes and empty video panels on slides 17, 20, 21, 22, 25 and 29, and enlarge the body text on the dense slides.

**Text**

SOURCING | SELECTING & WINNING | SUPPORTING | DRIVING RETURNS
HUMAN SUPPORT, SUPERCHARGED
BY TECH & INSIGHTS
High-EQ team at the core
The best founders value direct access to our small team of early-stage experts. They know we will be in their corner with empathy when it is tough, and that we will challenge them when it matters.
Our strength lies in asking the right questions at the right time, helping them see around corners, supporting through both highs and lows, and making the right introductions when they are most impactful.
Tech as the amplifier
We build tools that maximize our high-impact time with founders.
By leveraging our unique dataset across hundreds of companies’ journeys, we surface the right insights and benchmarks at the right moment, providing the right context for every decision and turning each interaction into sharper guidance and stronger outcomes.
Seedcamp have backed us from day 1, they bet on the founders, not product, traction, revenue, roadmaps, but on people. They have supported us through good and bad times, they told us to go hard when things were good and clipped us around the ear when needed. They nurtured community where we met other founders going through the exact same shit as we were, those founder to founder chats are the best relationships you ever build.
Gautam Rishi
Founder
The team that Reshma and Carlos have built have never strayed from their founding principles. Being available to founders and going above and beyond to support their success.
Adam Bird
Founder
Building Jarvis: our AI powered copilot
FUNDRAISING DECK 2025
17

**Layout**

Cream slide with a top navigation row, a large two-line left-aligned title, then a grid of boxes below. Left column has a wide outlined text card above two dark green testimonial cards side by side. Right column has an outlined text card above a large outlined video box with caption. A thin dark vertical line with a leaf icon runs along the right margin, with rotated footer text and page number beneath it.

**Visuals**

Two dark green rounded testimonial cards, each with a bright mint green horizontal stripe near the bottom, a cut-out portrait photo of a founder (Gautam Rishi, a smiling man with dark hair in a teal-blue sweatshirt; Adam Bird, a smiling grey-haired man in a light patterned shirt) and a white rounded logo tile (ONESHOT-style grey wordmark; Cronofy with a C icon). A large black rectangle (video placeholder) sits in the right outlined box. Three outlined rounded rectangles with a thin dark green stroke. A thin dark green vertical line on the right with a small dark green circle holding a bright green leaf icon at the top. Cream slide corners are rounded.

**Design**

Background: Solid cream #F7EEE5. Cream background #F7EEE5 with rounded slide corners. Titles in dark forest green #1E352D, wide geometric bold sans-serif (Syne-like extended grotesque) in uppercase for the main title, medium weight for card headings. Body text in light-weight Inter-style sans-serif in dark green #1E352D, about 1.9% of slide height. Testimonial cards use #1E352D fill with cream #F7EEE5 text, key phrases in medium weight, names in mint #02FDA8. Outlined cards have a 1px dark green stroke and large corner radii of about 2% of slide height. Navigation inactive items grey #A6A6A6, active item dark green. No shadows or gradients.

**Text**

SOURCING | SELECTING & WINNING | SUPPORTING | DRIVING RETURNS
WINNING WITH TALENT
Talent Advisory
1-to-1 mentorship, focused on building strong founding teams in the first 18 months. Founders also get access to our Talent Hub and our First Hires Playbook, which share best practices on building early teams.
Talent Network
We connect founders to a growing pool of top talent, supporting hires from CTOs, Head of Sales, through to entry-level operators.
Seedcamp Talent Pool: Candidates apply privately to join a list visible only to our founders.
Seedcamp Jobs Board: A live portal with 1,000+ open roles across the Seedcamp Nation, publicly accessible.
2,719
Job applicants via Seedcamp jobs board in last 12m
-----
100+
Direct intros a year
~10%
conversion to placements
Expert Operator Network
A curated group of high-performing operators who support founders on scaling challenges, from go-to-market advice to technical mentorship.
Seedcamp Talent Network
Welcome to the Seedcamp Talent Network, a space to help you find active and engaged talent, keen in joining an early stage startup. Simply click on a profile and request an introduction and our team will loop you in with them.
Welcome to First Hires
You've just raised capital, now it's time to build your founding team.
You've been instrumental, thank you for such an incredible search.
Etienne, Emma
You are a ninja in hiring and a great mentor for us.
Serban, Source.dev
source.dev
Seedcamp's talent function has been amazing in helping Prometheux in countless occasions. Their ability to match people and companies is a true superpower.
Adriano, Prometheux
Prometheux

SOURCING | SELECTING & WINNING | SUPPORTING | DRIVING RETURNS
UNLOCKING BD & US ACCESS
The Seedcamp economy
Portfolio companies sell to each other, partner together, and share GTM learnings.
We track which Seedcamp companies sell to which enterprises to foster peer-to-peer support and warm intros.
Through the Seedcamp Founders Pack, our companies access preferential deals with the most popular vendors.
Growing our US-based presence
Permanent presence in Miami and New York through Reshma (Managing Partner) and Hilary (Select strategy Partner).
Next Select strategy hire will be in New York to deepen our footprint following the launch of our second dedicated Select fund.
All core investment team members (associate level and above) travel to the US at least once a year to maintain investor and ecosystem relationships.
Supporting US expansion

30+ Seedcamp founders already based in the US share lessons on scaling from Europe, with an active NYC community at the core.

**Layout**

Cream slide with a top navigation row, large title with US flag at top left. Below, three stacked dark green rounded cards fill the left ~45% width; a cream outlined panel with heading and eight stacked logo pills sits in the middle; a black screenshot box with a chat header sits on the right. A thin vertical dark green line with a leaf icon runs near the right edge, with rotated footer text and page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

US flag icon (waving, rounded rectangle, red/white stripes with blue canton and white stars) beside the title. Eight white rounded-rectangle pills with outlines, each with a company logo: UiPath (orange square 'Ui' and orange 'Path'), Flow (black interlocking oval line icon and 'Flow'), 9fin (blue), GAIA (dark charcoal condensed letters), sense street (blue icon and black text on two lines), Metaview (black geometric icon with blue accent and black text), liveflow (blue), Orbital (orange cube icon with teal text). Right: a screenshot of a chat app header reading 'SC US Founders / 2 online' with a green circular avatar bearing a flag, video, phone and search icons, over a large black empty area. Green circular leaf icon on the right vertical line. Slide has rounded corners at the top.

**Design**

Background warm cream #F7EEE5. Title dark green #1E352D, bold wide geometric sans-serif (Cabinet/Syne-like), about 6.5% of slide height, uppercase. Card headings bright mint green #19E58C in a wide rounded display sans, about 3.5%. Body text cream #F7EEE5 in light grotesque sans (Inter/Manrope light), about 1.9%. Navigation in small uppercase light sans, active item dark green, others grey #A6A6A6. Cards dark green #1E352D with about 1.5% corner radius; middle panel cream with 0.15% dark green outline and rounded corners; logo pills white with thin dark green outline and small radius. No shadows or gradients.

**Text**

SOURCING | SELECTING & WINNING | SUPPORTING | DRIVING RETURNS
DRIVING FUNDRAISING SUCCESS
Shaping the Right Strategy
Founders rely on us for guidance on their fundraising approach.
They know we have a strong pulse on the funding market.
Our portfolio scale gives us a unique information-flow advantage (100-150 transactions a year).
Preparing to Win
Founders get access to our Fundraising Playbook.
We help structure a compelling narrative, build the deck, and set up the data room.
We run pitch mock sessions and give actionable feedback ahead of investor meetings.
Unlocking Tier-1 Access
Relationships compound over time: we have worked with nearly all tier-1 VCs across Europe and the US.
Our multiple touchpoints across these firms allow us to connect founders with the right partner at the right moment and to backchannel when needed.
Fundraising Playbook
1. General context
Fundraising is the one area where VCs always have more experience than even the most seasoned serial founders. They see tens of processes every year, while founders might run only a few in their lifetime. That asymmetry is important to recognise: it means preparation, discipline, and process matter more than instinct.
A fundraise should be treated as a focused campaign. Clear your calendar, align the founding team and existing investors on when to

**Layout**

Light cream slide with a breadcrumb nav at top left and a large all-caps title beneath. Left column holds three stacked dark green rounded cards with heading and body text; right column holds a Fundraising Playbook document screenshot above a boxed 4x4 grid of investor logo tiles. A thin vertical dark green line at far right with a leaf badge, rotated footer text and page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Right top: screenshot of a document page (white, with dark green header band and a bright green circular badge with a white leaf icon at top left); monospace text, a small scrollbar of grey ticks at right and a mouse cursor. Below it, a rounded-outline box containing a 4x4 grid of white tiles with investor logos: Balderton. (dark navy serif), Accel (dark serif), General Catalyst (black caps with purple-blue G icon), Founders Fund (red, teal and dark navy stacked bars above small caps text), NEA (navy and green letters), Sequoia (grey caps with green icon), Lightspeed (black serif with orange-red mark), Northzone (black caps), Index Ventures (red lines icon with dark text), a16z (dark grey), a black speech-bubble-like logo, Creandum (black spaced caps), USV (white on green square), Thrive Capital (black with red mark), LocalGlobe (black with red and navy dot icon), Kleiner Perkins (black icon with spaced caps). A dark green circle with a green leaf icon sits on the vertical line at top right.

**Design**

Background cream #F7EEE5. Title in dark green #1E352D, heavy extended geometric sans-serif caps. Card headings in bright mint green #00F28C in a rounded wide display sans; card body text in cream #F7EEE5 light sans. Cards fill #1E352D with rounded corners of about 1.5% radius. Nav in small grey #A6A6A6 sans with active item SUPPORTING in dark green. Logo tiles white with small radius; the investor box has a thin dark green outline with rounded corners. Playbook screenshot has a monospace bold title. No shadows or gradients.

### Slide 22

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential, though: several pages carry very small body text, long paragraphs and logo walls (slides 8, 20, 21, 31), and several pages show black redaction boxes or empty black video panels (slides 17, 20, 21, 22, 25, 29), which look unfinished.
- Suggested repair: Remove or replace the black redaction boxes and empty video panels on slides 17, 20, 21, 22, 25 and 29, and enlarge the body text on the dense slides.

**Layout**

Top nav row and large left-aligned title. Below, a left column of three dark green panels (two stat cards, fund timeline, advisor logos) and a right dark green panel with a Recent M&A's header, a 13-row logo list with connecting lines, and a row of four secondaries logos. A vertical dark line with a leaf icon sits at the right, with rotated footer text and the page number at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Left stats panel has two cream cards. The timeline panel has three rows of 11 cream pill shapes each (F2, F3, F4), with one mint green pill in F2 (7th), two in F3 (6th and 9th) and one in F4 (5th). Each green pill has a thin connector line down to its label, and black redaction bars sit beside or beneath the labels. The advisor row has three cream logo cards: menalo Advisors (orange M mark), founderpartners (diamond icon), Pura Advisory (green text). The right panel has 13 pairs of cream logo cards in two columns, each pair joined by a thin green horizontal line with mint dots at both ends. The left column holds Nordigen, Reinfer, skew, pace revenue, reply.ai, HelloFlow, libryo, UTOPIA, hopin, TaxScouts, pento, SURFBOARD, Ezra and WealthKernel. The right column holds GoCardless, UiPath, coinbase, FLYR, a Facebook-style icon pair, Truloo, ERM, shopify, RingCentral, taxfix, HiBob, dialpad, Function and Alpaca. Four cream cards below show the secondaries logos: PLEO, Revolut, 9fin and Cronofy. A circular dark green icon with a mint leaf sits on the vertical line at top right.

**Design**

Background: Solid cream #F7EEE5. Cream background #F7EEE5 with dark forest green #1E352D panels. Title is dark green bold uppercase geometric sans-serif (Cabinet Grotesk-like), about 6% of the slide height. Body text in panels is cream light sans-serif at about 1.9% of the slide height. Accents are mint green #00F5A0. Panels have rounded corners of about 1.5% with no shadows. The slide's top-right and bottom-right corners are rounded.

### Slide 23

**Text**

SEEDCAMP
Arcjet
Freqens
Murphy
.txt
An opportunity to invest

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark green slide with a horizontal neon-green band across the upper middle at about 40% height. Four cream polaroid-style photo cards of different sizes sit along the band: Arcjet cropped at the left edge, Freqens left of centre, Murphy centred-right and largest, .txt cropped at the right edge. Wordmark logo sits top left, a round leaf icon top right, and the headline is bottom left.

**Visuals**

A horizontal neon green (#02FDA8) band runs the full slide width behind the photos, passing in front of the photo areas so it crosses each photo at chest/face level. Four cream (#F7EEE5) rounded-corner cards with duotone green-tinted photos and captions. 1) Arcjet: cropped at left edge, close-up of a smiling man with short brown hair, rimless glasses and stubble in a blue quarter-zip fleece, against a blue-green leafy tinted background. 2) Freqens: three men standing outdoors, left in an orange-brown shirt, centre in a navy sweater with arms crossed, right in a grey jacket, against a teal tinted city skyline. 3) Murphy: two young men seated, left bearded with round glasses in a white t-shirt with hands clasped, right in a grey t-shirt and beige trousers, in front of a green tinted wall with an OKINAWA bonsai poster. 4) .txt: cropped at right edge, two bearded or dark-haired men with glasses, in dark t-shirt and light blue collared shirt, a third partially visible at far right in black cap and jacket, on a teal background with binary-code digits. Top-left SEEDCAMP wordmark in neon green; top-right a neon green circle with a small dark leaf/sprout icon.

**Design**

Background: Solid dark green #1E352D. Dark forest green background #1E352D. Neon mint green #02FDA8 for the logo, band, icon and the highlighted words 'to invest'. Cream #F7EEE5 for cards and the words 'An opportunity'. Card captions in dark green #1E352D in a rounded geometric sans-serif. Headline in a wide geometric sans-serif (Syne/Gilroy-like) medium weight at about 5.5% of slide height. Card corners rounded about 1% radius, photos with smaller rounded corners; no shadows; slide itself has rounded corners.

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Sets the two funds side by side in matching columns for thesis, ticket size, ownership and reserves, making the comparison direct.

**Text**

STRATEGY
WE’RE RAISING ACROSS 2 STRATEGIES TO CONTINUE / INVESTING IN EUROPE’S BEST FOUNDERS
Seedcamp VII
£165M/$220M
Core Strategy
~35 new companies/year / 100-120 companies / per fund
Ticket sizes: / Up to £1m ($1.3m)
5-10% target ownership
70% lead, 30% co-invest
40% follow-on reserve / backing top companies / through to and including / Series A
Thesis
Geo – Europe, focused / and selectively / Israel and the US
Type - High margin, / software-driven / businesses, predominantly / with short time-to market
Market - Large and / durable, with $10bn+ / outcome potential for all / investments
See more on slides 26-28
Seedcamp Nation II
£70M/$95M
Select Strategy
~8 new companies/year - / 25 companies per fund
Ticket sizes: / Avg $2-3m at Series B
33% follow-on reserve / backing companies / through to Exit
Thesis
90% - sourced from / Seedcamp portfolio
Up to 10% net new sourced / from broader Seedcamp / network
Seeking outperformance: / Top decile metrics, / demonstrated leadership / excellence, maturing GTM / process and roadmap to / category leadership

Seedcamp Nation II
Seedcamp VII
1st cheque
Seed
Series A
Series B
Series C
Series D
IPO
FUNDRAISING DECK 2025
24

**Layout**

Small section label top-left, large two-line left-aligned title below it. Two large rounded cards side by side fill the middle (dark green left, muted teal right), each with a heading, a cream amount box at top right, and two columns (strategy and thesis) of rows separated by thin lines with small arrows. A dotted-outline timeline panel spans the bottom. A vertical dark line with a leaf badge sits at the right edge, with a rotated footer text and page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Two rounded rectangle cards: dark green (#1E352D) on left and teal-grey (#507267) on right, each with a cream rounded box holding the fund size. Thin pale horizontal divider lines between rows and a thin vertical divider between the two columns in each card. Small left-pointing arrow icons at the right end of each row. A vertical dark green line at the right edge with a circular dark green badge containing a bright green leaf icon near the top. Bottom dotted-outline rounded panel containing a stage timeline: a dark green bar labelled Seedcamp VII spanning 1st cheque to Seed, then a semi-transparent green-grey bar continuing across Seed and fading into Series A, with a thin white arrow pointing right; a teal bar labelled Seedcamp Nation II over Series B, with a left-pointing arrow extending into the Series A region and a right-pointing arrow extending through Series C and Series D in progressively lighter grey-green bars. Stage labels in bold black beneath: 1st cheque, Seed, Series A, Series B, Series C, Series D, IPO.

**Design**

Background: Solid cream #F7EEE5 with rounded slide corners. Cream background (#F7EEE5). Title in dark green (#1E352D), bold extended geometric sans-serif in all caps. Card headings in bright mint green (#00F5A0), large extended sans-serif medium weight. Body text in cream (#F7EEE5), light sans-serif (Inter-like) with some bold phrases. Cards have rounded corners about 1% of width, flat fill, no shadows. Amount boxes cream with rounded corners and dark green bold text. Timeline labels in bold condensed-looking sans-serif.

### Slide 25

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential, though: several pages carry very small body text, long paragraphs and logo walls (slides 8, 20, 21, 31), and several pages show black redaction boxes or empty black video panels (slides 17, 20, 21, 22, 25, 29), which look unfinished.
- Suggested repair: Remove or replace the black redaction boxes and empty video panels on slides 17, 20, 21, 22, 25 and 29, and enlarge the body text on the dense slides.

**Text**

CORE - SEEDCAMP VII
SELECT - SEEDCAMP NATION II
A MODEL THAT HAS CONVICTION IN EARLY, / AND CAN CAPTURE THE OUTLIERS
We aim to lead 70% of our investments, / typically in rounds of up to $3-3.5m, / resulting in 7–10% ownership in target / deals.
Where we lead, our comparatively low / ownership leaves room to partner with / leading VCs, micro-funds, and Angels.

**Layout**

Title at top left spanning two lines. Below, two panels side by side: a left outlined cream box with four paragraphs separated by thin rules, and a larger dark green panel on the right holding the chart and a caption card. A vertical dark green line with a round leaf icon runs down the right edge, with a rotated footer text and page number at the bottom right. Breadcrumb tabs sit at top left.

**Visuals**

Bell-curve style line chart in bright mint green on a cream card inside a dark green panel. The line has a tall peak (about 85% of chart height) over the $10-13M position, dips to a trough near $15-20M, rises to a small second peak over $25M+ (about 15% of chart height), then tails off toward the right. Two horizontal range lines with dot end markers sit above the curve: the left one spans from $6M to about $15-20M (Lead / Co-Lead) and the right one spans from about $25M to the right edge (Follow). A vertical dashed mint line drops from the small peak through the $25M+ label to the memories.ai logo. X-axis labels in mint on dark green: $6M, $10-13M, $15-20M, $25M+, 1st round Valuation. A black rectangle sits in the caption card (blank or redacted logo). A black rounded-pill memories.ai logo with a white geometric mark and white lowercase wordmark sits at the right of the caption card. A small dark green circle with a mint leaf icon sits on the vertical line at top right.

**Design**

Background: Solid cream #F7EEE5. Warm cream background #F7EEE5 with rounded slide corners. Dark forest green #1E352D for title, labels and panels. Mint accent #02FDA8 for the curve and axis labels. Title in a bold, wide geometric grotesque sans-serif (Syne/Gilroy-like) in uppercase, about 6% of slide height. Body in a light/regular geometric sans-serif (Inter/Syne-like) about 2.4% of slide height, in near-black. Left box has a 1px dark outline with a corner radius of about 1.5%. Right panel has rounded corners of about 1%. Inner cards are cream with rounded corners. Thin grey rules divide the paragraphs. No shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Small grey-green header tabs at top left, then a large three-line left-aligned title across the top 55% of the width. Below, four dark-green rounded panels sit side by side in the lower left, each holding a flag header box and white-cream cards with logos. A large green map of Europe fills the right side. Vertical rotated footer text and page number at the far right.

**Visuals**

Four columns of dark green (#1E352D) rounded panels with cream inner cards. Column 1: one tall card with UK flag header and 22 startup logos. Column 2: Israel flag header with 4 logos (Botika, Knostic, EyeContact, appwrite); France flag header with 14 logos (Sorare, txt, Maze, Prelude, a multicolour logo, Argil, Flagcat, DoorFeed, liveblocks, yavin, rayon). Column 3: flags Czech Republic, Estonia, Romania, Poland with 6 logos (ezra, UiPath, TaxScouts, Ramp, Wise, Rossum); flags Germany and Switzerland with 12 logos (wefox, Grover, buynomics, SE3, Witty Machines, cakewalk, Kraka, tl;dv, Zenline AI, verisian, AskUI, ENAPI). Column 4: flags Spain, Portugal, Italy with 3 logos (Murphy, Lodgify, MicroSignals); flags Sweden, Norway, Denmark, Finland with 7 logos (Pleo, Light, HelloFlow, medusa, rerun.io, spektr, fullview, pento). Large sage-green map of Europe (about 60% opacity) fills the right half, running off the top, right and bottom edges. A thin vertical dark green line runs near the right edge at x about 95.5% with a small dark green circle containing a white leaf icon at top, about y 9.6%. Rounded slide corners.

**Design**

Background: Cream #F7EEE5 with a sage-green (#457967 at 60%) map of Europe on the right. Warm cream background #F7EEE5. Title in dark green #1E352D, heavy geometric sans-serif capitals (similar to Gilroy/Syne Bold), about 6.5% of slide height, tight line spacing. Header tabs small sans-serif: CORE in dark green, SELECT in light grey #BFBFBF. Panels dark green #1E352D with rounded corners about 1.5%, cream inner cards with rounded corners. ### Slide 27

**Text**

CORE - SEEDCAMP VII
SELECT - SEEDCAMP NATION II
WE CAPITALISE ON THE BREAKOUT STARS
WITH OUR FOLLOW-ON STRATEGY
The fund model breaks down the portfolio into categories that determine follow-on allocations.
We assess decisions based on our ownership, exit outcomes, company performance and conviction. We keep reserves consistently across the cohorts of investments, so those raising later in the fund cycle are not penalised.
Winners - able to receive up to $3.5m to defend & increase ownership through Series A
Capital available
No allocation available
We have successfully allocated higher amounts to our top ranked companies
F3
F4
F5
20%
30%
40%
50%
60%
% of follow-on budget the top 20% of companies have received per fund.
Backing 100+ companies means we also see late bloomers in the fund cycle, further driving returns
9fin
HIGHLAND EUROPE
9fin backed in 2017, raised in 2026 at a $1bn+ valuation.
Flow
SEQUOIA
Flow backed in 2017, raised $25m Series A from Sequoia in 2025.

**Layout**

Cream slide with small breadcrumb at top left, large two-line uppercase title below, and two intro paragraphs left-aligned. Three equal rounded cards sit side by side across the lower half (dot matrix card, bar chart card, logo card). A thin vertical dark green line runs down the right edge with a round leaf icon at top.

**Visuals**

Left card: a grid of dots, 5 columns by 18 rows. Row 1 all bright green; row 2 has one green dot then four grey-green; rows 3-4 grey-green; rows 5-8 dark green; rows 9-12 pale yellow; rows 13-16 amber, with the first two dots of row 16 amber and the other three red; rows 17-19 red. Between the dots and text are two thin green up arrows. Middle card: horizontal bar chart on a muted green card with title in a cream box. Bars bright green #02FDA8: F3 ends at about 47%, F4 at about 51%, F5 at about 55%. X axis 20% to 60% with vertical light grey gridlines at 20, 30, 40, 50, 60%, left axis line. No printed values. Right card: four cream rounded logo tiles: 9fin wordmark in blue, Highland Europe with blue square H logo, Flow with an interlaced circle icon, Sequoia in green with a tree-ring icon. A thin line divides the top pair from the bottom pair. Round dark green leaf icon with green outline at top right on the vertical line.

**Design**

Background warm cream #F7EEE5. Title and intro text in dark green #1E352D, wide extended geometric bold sans (Syne/Gilroy-like extended) for title, medium-weight rounded sans for intro. Cards dark green #1E352D, middle card muted green #507267 with cream title box. Card body text cream #F7EEE5 light sans (Inter-like). Accent bright mint #02FDA8. Cards have small corner radius, no shadows. Title about 7% of slide height, intro about 2.7%, card text about 2.3%.

**Layout**

Two-part layout: left column (about 4% to 33% width) holds breadcrumb at top, a large three-line title, a bold subheading, a grey paragraph, a thin divider, a small caption and a 4x3 logo grid panel. Right side has a 2-column by 3-row grid of equal metric cards (x 38-91%). A thin vertical dark green line with a leaf badge runs at the far right, with a rotated footer and page number below.

**Visuals**

Six metric cards in a 2x3 grid, alternating fills: row 1 dark green (left) and cream outlined (right); row 2 cream outlined (left) and dark green (right); row 3 dark green (left) and cream outlined (right). Each card has a small icon at top-left and a thin diagonal up-right arrow at top-right. Icons: stacked coins (bright green on dark), coins with a small gear/clock (dark on cream), office building with bar chart (dark on cream), bar chart with rising arrow (green on dark), network/nodes graph (green on dark), pie chart (dark on cream). A dark green rounded panel at the bottom left holds a 4x3 grid of cream rounded tiles with company logos: Wise (dark green italic wordmark), Pleo (black), UiPath (orange), synthesia (dark blue with icon), Metaview (black with triangle icon), maze (dark with loop icon), wefox (purple), 9fin (blue), sorare (black with circle icon), Primer (dark with orange icon), yonder (dark with Y icon), Function (faded orange with sphere icon). A thin horizontal grey-green divider line sits under the paragraph. At right edge: vertical dark green line with a small dark green circle containing a bright green leaf icon near the top.

**Design**

Background: Solid cream #F7EEE5. Warm cream background #F7EEE5 with dark forest green #1E352D for title, text and filled cards, and bright mint green #1EF0A0 for numbers and icons on dark cards. Title in heavy uppercase wide geometric sans (Syne/Cabinet-like bold, tight) at about 6% of slide height; subheading in the same bold wide sans at about 2.8%; body and card captions in a clean grotesque sans (Inter) regular. Cards have small corner radius (about 1%), dark cards solid fill and cream cards with 1px dark green outline. Logo panel has rounded corners. No shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Small breadcrumb at top-left, large two-line title below, subtitle line, then a 4x2 grid of dark green cards. A thin vertical dark green line sits at the right with a leaf icon, and rotated footer text and page number at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Eight dark green rounded cards, each with a white-cream rounded logo box (9fin, Metaview, Ben, synthesia, maze, Function, yonder, Lindus Health) and 1-2 investor logo tiles at the bottom right (Highland Europe, Spark Capital, G/ (GV), atomico, NEA, Accel, Felicis, Redpoint, rtp global, LocalGlobe, Balderton). Small white arrow bullets sit next to the funding rows, and the funding amounts are covered by black redaction boxes in the image. A round dark green icon with a white leaf sits at top right on a vertical dark green line.

**Design**

Background: Solid cream #F7EEE5 with rounded slide corners. Warm cream background #F7EEE5; title in dark green #1E352D, wide geometric bold sans-serif (Syne/Gilroy-like) in capitals; cards #1E352D with rounded corners of about 8px; card text cream/white in a light Inter-like sans-serif; logo tiles cream #F7EEE5 with small radius; no shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Two-column layout. Left column holds a large all-caps title at top, a subtitle, three arrow bullets and a large callout headline with an arrow pointing right toward the table. Right column is a large dark green rounded panel containing a chart title, a clustered bar chart, a legend and a cream rounded table. A breadcrumb nav sits at top left, a three-part footnote row runs along the bottom, a vertical line with a leaf icon runs down the right side, with rotated footer text and page number 30 at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Clustered bar chart with 5 categories (Fund III (2014), Fund IV (2017), Fund V (2020), Fund VI (2023), Fund VII (2026)) on a y-axis from 0.0% to 7.0% in 1.0% steps with light horizontal gridlines. Two series per category. Post Initial (bright green) about 2.8%, 4.6%, 5.4%, 6.1%, 7.2%. Post Series A (grey-green) about 2.3%, 3.8%, 4.9%, 6.0%, 7.1%. Legend with two cream rounded chips with green and grey-green swatches. Dashed bright green vertical lines run from beneath the Fund IV, V, VI and VII categories down into the table columns. Cream rounded table with two rows (1x Pro-rata, 2x Pro-rata) and four value columns separated by thin vertical lines and a thin horizontal divider. Black horizontal arrow points right from the callout text. Thin dark vertical line at the right edge with a small dark green circle holding a green leaf icon. Thin horizontal rule above the footnotes at the left and small vertical separators between footnotes.

**Design**

Background: Cream #F7EEE5 with black showing in the rounded corners. Cream background #F7EEE5 with a rounded-corner slide edge showing black behind. Titles and body in dark green #1E352D; chart title and chart accents in bright green #02FDA8; grey-green bars #507267. Wide geometric sans-serif (Syne-like/Gilroy extended) for headings, bold all-caps for main title, medium weight for the callout and subtitle. Body bullets in a light grey-green sans (about #1E352D at 80% opacity). Chart labels in Arial. Panel has rounded corners about 1.5% radius, no shadow.

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential, though: several pages carry very small body text, long paragraphs and logo walls (slides 8, 20, 21, 31), and several pages show black redaction boxes or empty black video panels (slides 17, 20, 21, 22, 25, 29), which look unfinished.
- What would change the score: Cut the paragraph copy and logo walls on slides 8, 12, 20, 21 and 31 down to one claim each, and make the metric sets on slide 6 consistent across funds.

**Layout**

Full-width cream slide with a thin vertical dark-green rule at the right (about x 95.6%) carrying a round green leaf icon near the top. Small breadcrumb at top left, large two-line left-aligned title, a two-line intro paragraph, then a large dark-green rounded panel across the lower 60% holding a 7-column by 2-row logo grid with header row and left row labels. Vertical footer text and page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Dark green rounded rectangle panel (about x 3.6%, y 32.5%, w 90.2%, h 61.2%) containing a rounded outlined table with thin darker green divider lines: header row of seven category names, a 'Success stories' row and a 'Snapshot of companies' row. Each company is shown as a small rounded off-white/cream tile with its logo in the company's own colours: Success stories row has 2 tiles in Fintech, 1 in Enterprise, 2 in Health/Bio, 2 in AI, 'Coming soon...' placeholder tiles in Dev Tools and Security, 1 in Outlying. Snapshot row is a 2-wide by 6-high grid of tiles in each of the first four categories and Dev Tools/Security/Outlying has 1-wide... actually 2 columns in the first four and a single column of 6 tiles in Dev Tools, Security and Outlying. Round dark green circle with white-green leaf icon at top right on the vertical rule. Small vertical rule at the far left of the breadcrumb.

**Design**

Background: Cream #F7EEE5 with rounded corners and a thin dark green vertical line at right. Cream background #F7EEE5; title and paragraph in dark green #1E352D; panel fill #1E352D; category labels and row labels in bright mint green #19E58A; extended wide geometric sans-serif (Syne/Unbounded-like) bold uppercase title about 5.5% of slide height; paragraph medium-weight about 2.6% height; breadcrumb small sans-serif. Tiles are off-white #F7EEE5 with small corner radius about 6px, no shadow. Panel corner radius about 1.5%.

### Slide 32

**Text**

WHY INVEST
Best known / in Europe
Proven / returns
First cheque / focus
Trusted / by the best
We were Europe’s first seed fund, set up in 2007 to invest across the continent. Our entrenched brand and first-mover advantage continue to compound year after year
We remain disciplined to a model with a proven track record of delivering DPI, and we are proud to prioritise timely investor returns.
We exclusively back companies as first-cheque investors, staying hyper-focused at this initial stage in Europe.
With our Select fund, we have the unfair advantage of knowing companies for 3–5 years before backing them again, helping us double down on the global category leaders of tomorrow.
We are the most experienced early-stage investing team and partnership in Europe, backed by some of the best investors in the world who continue to support each of our funds.
Over 100 of our founders also invested in our last oversubscribed fund, highlighting the strength of the Seedcamp flywheel
FUNDRAISING DECK 2025
32

**Layout**

Title at top left with a small diagonal arrow beside it. Four equal dark green rounded cards in a row beneath, each with a two-line green heading and icon at top right, and an inset cream rounded panel with body text in the lower part. A thin vertical line with a leaf badge runs down the right side, with rotated footer text and page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Four small bright green icons at the top right of each card: a building/city icon (Best known in Europe), a stack of coins (Proven returns), a connected-nodes network icon (First cheque focus), and a people/group with speech block icon (Trusted by the best). A small arrow icon (diagonal down-right) after the title. A dark green circle with a bright green leaf icon sits on a thin vertical dark green line at the right edge. Slide corners are rounded.

**Design**

Background: Solid cream #F7EEE5 with rounded slide corners. Cream background #F7EEE5. Title in dark green #1E352D, bold geometric sans-serif in capitals, large (about 7% of slide height). Card headings in bright green #00E88F in a rounded geometric sans-serif (Cabinet Grotesk/Syne-like), medium weight, about 3.3% of height. Body text in light-weight grey-green #5A5F5A sans-serif (Inter Light), about 2.4% of height with generous line spacing. Cards are #1E352D with approx 1.5% corner radius; inner panels cream with smaller radius. No shadows or gradients.

### Slide 33

**Text**

SEEDCAMP
JOIN THE
SEEDCAMP
NATION

**Layout**

Two-panel split on a cream frame. A rounded dark green panel fills the left ~58% with the Seedcamp wordmark top-left and a leaf icon top-right, a left-aligned three-line headline in the lower-middle, and a bright green ticker band across the lower part. A rounded team photo fills the right ~40%, and the green band continues behind the people.

**Visuals**

Right: a colour photograph of about 20 smiling people standing close together in a group on a sunlit London street in front of a brown brick building with white-framed sash windows, a black lamp post, a drainpipe, a Union Jack flag and a 'Dean Street W1' sign. Shot at eye level, group in the lower half, bright daylight with cast shadows on the road, warm brown and navy/black clothing. The photo has rounded corners and is boxed. The bright green band passes behind the group, visible between and above the legs. Left: a Seedcamp wordmark logo in mint green and a small mint green circle with a white-outlined dark leaf icon. A bright green horizontal ticker band with black repeated text spans the panel width.

**Design**

Background: Cream #F7EEE5 frame with a dark green #1E352D rounded panel on the left and a photo on the right. Cream frame #F7EEE5; left panel dark forest green #1E352D with large rounded corners (about 2% of slide height). Headline in a wide, bold geometric sans-serif (Syne/Unbounded-like extended grotesque): 'JOIN THE' in cream #F7EEE5, 'SEEDCAMP NATION' in mint green #02FDA8. Headline about 9% of slide height per line, tight leading. Ticker text is a regular sans-serif (Inter-like), uppercase, black, about 1.5% of slide height, with bullet separators. Logo is a condensed serif wordmark in mint green. No shadows or gradients.
